- **long-range-business-plan-architect**: Build or refine an interactive long-range business plan (3-10 years) using staged user interviews, evidence-backed research, scenario modeling, and execution roadmapping. Use when a user asks for a business plan, long-term strategy, strategic clarity, business-model stress testing, or discovery of missing niche expert skills/agents needed to execute the plan. ([whodaniel/The-New-Fuse](https://github.com/whodaniel/The-New-Fuse/tree/HEAD/.agent/skills/long-range-business-plan-architect) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **long-range-continuity**: Track and verify continuity facts that must remain consistent across distant feature-film sequences. ([badgids/Story-Film-Skills](https://github.com/badgids/Story-Film-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/long-range-continuity) · ★10 · 2 copies)
- **long-range-dependence-financial-markets**: Empirical investigation of long-range dependence (LRD) in financial markets and evaluation of deep generative models' ability to reproduce such temporal structures across equity, commodity, and energy sectors. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/finance/long-range-dependence-financial-markets) · ★4)
- **long-range-dna-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when comparing fine-tuned DNA foundation models against task-specific expert models on genomic prediction tasks with long-range dependencies (e.g., TISP, contact map prediction, eQTL, enhancer-target gene, regulatory sequence activity), or when reproducing PCC/SCC/AUROC benchmark tables. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/long-range-dna-benchmark-evaluation))
- **long-range-genomic-dependency-benchmarking**: Use when comparing fine-tuned DNA foundation models against task-specific expert architectures on genomic prediction tasks whose input sequences span long ranges (up to 1,000,000 bp), e.g. eQTL binary classification over 450,000-bp inputs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/long-range-genomic-dependency-benchmarking))
- **long-range-genomic-sequence-modeling**: Use when evaluating or comparing sequence models on genomic prediction tasks with long-range dependencies (e.g., enhancer-target gene prediction, eQTL, contact map), or when deciding whether a DNA foundation model can replace a task-specific expert model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/long-range-genomic-sequence-modeling))
- **long-range-precision-fires-deep-sensing-integration-cell**: Support U.S. warfighter planning and decision support for Long Range Precision Fires Deep Sensing Integration Cell. Use when missions require Long Range Precision Fires Deep Sensing Integration Cell planning, cross-domain options, and protocol-aware staff outputs. ([zwright8/OpenClaw-Code](https://github.com/zwright8/OpenClaw-Code/tree/HEAD/skills/warfighter/long-range-precision-fires-deep-sensing-integration-cell) · ★1)
- **long-range-regulatory-mechanism-annotation**: Use when a structural variant from a congenital abnormality patient lacks a coding explanation and you need to identify the affected gene via a long-range regulatory mechanism in a phenotype-matched cell type. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/long-range-regulatory-mechanism-annotation))
- **long-read**: Answer a bounded question over long material with exact evidence and visible coverage limits. Use when asked to material exceeds comfortable context and a focused question needs evidence. ([michaeljabbour/michaeljabbour.github.io](https://github.com/michaeljabbour/michaeljabbour.github.io/tree/HEAD/compatibility/sites/amplifier-skill-long-read/skills/long-read))
- **long-read-16s-consensus-accuracy-assessment**: Use when you have full-length 16S rRNA consensus sequences from a nanopore (ONT MinION) mock-community amplicon run with known expected OTU references, and you need per-OTU and average consensus accuracy numbers to validate a de novo consensus workflow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-16s-consensus-accuracy-assessment))
- **long-read-adapter-trimming**: Use when raw Nanopore long-read FASTQ data is about to enter de novo genome or plasmid assembly (e.g. Unicycler hybrid assembly in NanoGalaxy) and native or supplied adapter sequences must be removed first. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-adapter-trimming))
- **long-read-alignment**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-/tree/HEAD/Skills/Genomics/long-read-sequencing/long-read-alignment) · ★36 · 3 copies)
- **long-read-alignment-bam-preparation**: Use when a strain-oblivious metagenome assembly exists (or is being built with metaFlye) and Strainberry requires a coordinate-sorted, indexed long-read alignment BAM paired with an indexed FASTA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/long-read-alignment-bam-preparation))
- **long-read-alignment-benchmarking**: Use when comparing computational cost (wall-clock runtime, CPU hours, peak memory) of TE callers on PacBio or ONT long-read BAM files, or when reproducing the sTELLeR vs TLDR/xTEA efficiency claims on GIAB trio data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-alignment-benchmarking--262eb8e03b2be2a0))
- **long-read-alignment-benchmarking**: Use when you need to quantitatively compare long-read mappers on synthetic Illumina/PacBio/ONT reads whose true genomic origins are known, using precision/recall of mapping location and per-base accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-alignment-benchmarking--15155cb5ecf86b78))
- **long-read-alignment-command-profiling**: Use when benchmarking a streamed minimap2-&gt;HLAminer long-read command: wrap it in GNU time -v to capture wall-clock runtime and peak RAM on ONT/PacBio WGS FASTQ inputs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-alignment-command-profiling))
- **long-read-alignment-identity-extraction**: Use when invMap inversion-call outputs must be compared against other long-read mapper call sets to isolate invMap-only calls and record their alignment identity, length, and anchors, e.g. the novel 231 bp inversion on NA12878 data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-alignment-identity-extraction))
- **long-read-alignment-identity-qv-calculation**: Use when you need an alignment-based, reference-independent-of-sequencing accuracy metric comparing polishing pipelines on HiFi assemblies, using a high-confidence reference split into pseudo-long reads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-alignment-identity-qv-calculation))
- **long-read-alignment-of-base-converted-nanopore-reads**: Use when aligning enzymatically base-converted nanopore long reads (nanoEM/EMseq FASTQ, e.g. from DRA011237) where C-to-T/G-to-A conversion must be mirrored in the reference to recover high mapping rates over default minimap2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-alignment-of-base-converted-nanopore-reads))
- **long-read-alignment-parameter-tuning**: Use when running sTELLeR on PacBio/ONT long-read BAM or CRAM data to call non-reference TE insertions and you must choose sensitivity thresholds (supporting reads, mapping quality, max reads) appropriate to sample coverage and read style. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-alignment-parameter-tuning))
- **long-read-alignment-processing**: Use when raw long-read sequencing data (PacBio or ONT) must be turned into a sorted, indexed BAM aligned to hg19/mm10 so that breakpoint detection and rearrangement annotation can be performed in AgileStructure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-alignment-processing))
- **long-read-alignment-quality-control**: Use when you need to generate, inspect, or validate the interactive HTML QC report of a scywalker long-read single-cell (10x ONT or PacBio) run from its BAM and per-cell count outputs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-alignment-quality-control))
- **long-read-alignment-streaming-hla-prediction**: Use when HLA-I/II allele predictions are needed from raw uncorrected long nanopore or PacBio WGS reads, especially at low (~10x) coverage, without storing bulky alignment files. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-alignment-streaming-hla-prediction))
- **long-read-alignment-to-reference-genome**: Use when HiFi (CCS) long reads need to be aligned to GRCh38 as the evidence layer for structural variant discovery, e.g. before running sawfish discover. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-alignment-to-reference-genome--4ea4933e56b00e2a))
- **long-read-alignment-to-reference-genome**: Use when raw ONT or PacBio long-read sequencing data (FASTQ) must be mapped to GRCh38 to generate haplotype-resolved BAM alignments for genome-wide analysis such as TRiCoLOR TR calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-alignment-to-reference-genome--2639f247fab7d1dd))
- **long-read-alignment-to-reference-genome**: Use when paired tumor/control ONT or PacBio FASTQ data (e.g., COLO829/COLO829BL from ENA PRJEB27698) must be aligned to GRCh38 before somatic SV detection with nanomonsv. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-alignment-to-reference-genome--52cc25a3f04e1f38))
- **long-read-alignment-to-seed-sequences**: Use when you have long-read FASTA files and a uniform-length seed FASTA (unique numeric IDs, excluding 0) and need seed-to-read alignment files in SAM format to build a TULIP seed graph. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-alignment-to-seed-sequences))
- **long-read-alignment-transcript-model-collapsing**: Use when full-length non-chimeric (FLNC) Iso-Seq reads must be aligned to a reference genome and reduced to a non-redundant gene/isoform annotation in BED12 format. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-alignment-transcript-model-collapsing))
- **long-read-allele-prediction**: Use when you have raw uncorrected WGS long-read FASTQ (ONT PromethION or PacBio) and need HLA-I/-II allele predictions at group/allele resolution in a single command, without persisting SAM/BAM files. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-allele-prediction))
- **long-read-allelotyping**: Use when you have a sorted, indexed BAM/CRAM of long error-prone reads (PacBio SMRT or Oxford Nanopore) aligned to an indexed FASTA reference and need to determine, for each read at a candidate SNV, which allele it supports and with what quality. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-allelotyping))
- **long-read-amplicon-error-profiling**: Use when validating that simulated HiFi amplicon reads reproduce the error (edit distance) distributions of real sequencing data for mock communities such as Zymo/Titan-1, ATCC/16S, or Phylotag/16S. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/long-read-amplicon-error-profiling))
- **long-read-amplicon-otu-clustering**: Use when evaluating whether direct de novo OTU clustering of full-length nanopore 16S amplicon reads at 97% similarity yields accurate richness estimates for mock communities, or when benchmarking a partition-based clustering method like nanoClust against a direct-clustering baseline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-amplicon-otu-clustering))
- **long-read-amplicon-sequence-analysis**: Use when raw noisy ONT MinION 2D/1D2 amplicon reads from a mock or mixed microbial community must be converted into high-accuracy full-length 16S rRNA gene consensus sequences without reference-based mapping, and when residual errors need error-mode attribution (e.g., homopolymer deletions). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-amplicon-sequence-analysis))
- **long-read-anchor-matching**: Use when calling targeted macrohaplotypes from a read-reference alignment BAM and per-locus variant configure files (STR anchor table, InDel BED, SNP panel) and you need to decide which reads belong to which targeted locus before allele calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-anchor-matching))
- **long-read-arg-read-screening**: Use when quality-controlled Nanopore/PacBio metagenomic reads must be screened for ARG-carrying reads before species-resolved host assignment, and the identity threshold must adapt to platform-specific sequencing error. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-arg-read-screening))
- **long-read-assembly**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/analysis/long-read-assembly) · ★745)
- **long-read-assembly**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/AI-Agentic-Skills-by-Dr.-Mia](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/AI-Agentic-Skills-by-Dr.-Mia/tree/HEAD/Skills/Genomics/genome-assembly/long-read-assembly) · ★7)
- **long-read-assembly**: Assemble bacterial genomes from long-read sequencing data (ONT/PacBio). This skill implements the consensus assembly paradigm to resolve repeats and produce near-complete circular chromosomes and plasmids, integrating quality filtering and depth downsampling. Builds on the upstream read-qc-trimming skill. Has explicit ask-user stop points (SP10, SP11, SP12) that fire only when evidence is ambiguous. ([cheahhl814/bacterial-genome-analysis](https://github.com/cheahhl814/bacterial-genome-analysis/tree/HEAD/assembly/long-read-assembly) · ★1)
- **long-read-assembly-amr-profiling**: Use when the goal is to detect resistance genes and chromosomal point mutations in bacterial isolates from Nanopore long-read data, or to reproduce AMR gene-count comparisons between an assembly-based pipeline and a published study. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-assembly-amr-profiling))
- **long-read-assembly-polishing**: Use when you need to test whether basecalled FASTQ reads (e.g., from a nanopore signal simulator or real ONT run) yield near-perfect bacterial genome assemblies, judged by QUAST mismatches per 100 kbp and genome fraction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-assembly-polishing))
- **long-read-assembly-quality-assessment**: Use when comparing mis-scaffolding between two long-read assemblies (e.g., HiFiCCL-Hifiasm vs Hifiasm from ultra-low-coverage HG002/NA19240 HiFi reads) scaffolded to chromosomes with RagTag, where inter-chromosomal synteny links &gt;100 kb quantify mis-scaffolding. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-assembly-quality-assessment))
- **long-read-assembly-sample-processing**: Use when you have per-sample long-read assemblies (e.g., ~5× HiFi human assemblies from HiFiCCL-Hifiasm or Hifiasm) that must be pooled, made name-unique, and fed into minigraph pangenome graph construction for bubble-level precision/recall evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-assembly-sample-processing))
- **long-read-assembly-structural-quality-assessment**: Use when a new de novo assembly (e.g. nanopore/TULIP scaffolds) must be validated against an existing draft genome to determine mutually missing sequence, unmatched scaffold fractions, and structural improvement. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-assembly-structural-quality-assessment))
- **long-read-bam-block-parsing**: Use when you have a BAM of assembled contigs aligned to a reference (e.g. minimap2 alignments of VolcanoSV diploid assembly output) and need to extract large INS/DEL SV signatures from split-alignment geometry. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-bam-block-parsing))
- **long-read-barcode-demultiplexing**: Use when demultiplexing PacBio HiFi/FLNC long reads (e.g., HIT-scISOseq concatenated cDNA) into per-cell reads, or when benchmarking barcode assignment accuracy against a ground-truth spike-in such as SIRV-Set 4. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-barcode-demultiplexing))
- **long-read-barcode-umi-extraction**: Use when demultiplexing noisy long-read single-cell RNA-seq reads or 10x Chromium/Visium short reads where barcodes/UMIs must be extracted, corrected, and assigned against a whitelist (barcode_list.txt). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/long-read-barcode-umi-extraction))
- **long-read-basecalling-and-alignment**: Use when starting from ONT POD5 signal files that must be basecalled and mapped (e.g. ENCODE dRNA replicates) before modification detection or transcript quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-basecalling-and-alignment))
- **long-read-benchmark-table-reporting**: Use when benchmarking long-read (nanopore PromethION) whole-genome data aligned with multiple aligners and you need comparable read-length statistics and MAPQ-filtered genome coverage fractions per aligner. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-benchmark-table-reporting))
- **long-read-bisulfite-style-methylation-analysis**: Use when you have CT- and GA-converted nanopore reads already mapped to a converted reference with alignments selected, and you need per-CpG methylation frequencies genome-wide. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-bisulfite-style-methylation-analysis))
- **long-read-candidate-locus-pileup-detection**: Use when calling SNVs from ONT long-read BAM alignments and you need to detect candidate variant loci via read pileup with known-variant background stratification before genotyping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-candidate-locus-pileup-detection))
- **long-read-candidate-region-consumption**: Use when a long-read mapper must convert coarse stage-I seed-hit clusters (candidate regions) into precise alignment anchors for downstream anchor chaining (LCSk), particularly for error-prone MinION reads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-candidate-region-consumption))
- **long-read-ccs-read-generation**: Use when raw PacBio Sequel II subreads.bam files from short-insert single-cell isoform libraries (e.g., HIT-scISOseq, ScISOr-Seq) must be converted to ccs.bam before FLNC classification, especially when default CCS settings would discard the short ~1.5–5 kb inserts typical of scRNA cDNA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-ccs-read-generation))
- **long-read-circrna-consensus-sequence-calling**: Use when nanopore long-read data generated from rolling-circle-amplified circRNAs must be converted into corrected consensus sequences, high-confidence BSJs, and full-length circRNA isoforms (isocirc.out/bed/stats outputs). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-circrna-consensus-sequence-calling))
- **long-read-circular-rna-isoform-characterization**: Use when nanopore long-read sequencing of RCA-amplified circRNA products (FASTA/FASTQ) must be converted into a catalog of high-confidence full-length circRNA isoforms and back-splice junctions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-circular-rna-isoform-characterization))
- **long-read-circular-rna-pipeline-execution**: Use when processing isoCirc long-read FASTA/FASTQ data (RCA + nanopore) against a reference genome and GTF annotation to obtain per-isoform BSJ counts and full-length circRNA isoforms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-circular-rna-pipeline-execution))
- **long-read-compressed-representation**: Use when assembling large genomes (e.g., 3 Gbp H. sapiens) from long erroneous 3GS reads (PacBio/MinION) plus accurate short-read contigs, and base-level all-vs-all long-read overlap is computationally prohibitive. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-compressed-representation))
- **long-read-compression-via-kmer-anchoring**: Use when hybrid-assembling genomes with DBG2OLC: raw long 3GS reads must be converted to compact contig-identifier lists using accurate short-read DBG contigs before overlap, layout, and consensus. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-compression-via-kmer-anchoring))
- **long-read-consensus-error-quantification**: Use when evaluating consensus-sequence quality of third-generation long reads — specifically when raw ONT/PacBio read error rates must be compared against SPOA/POA consensus error rates to compute a percentage error reduction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-consensus-error-quantification))
- **long-read-consensus-generation**: Use when error-prone ONT/PacBio reads spanning candidate repetitive regions must be collapsed into haplotype-specific low-error consensus sequences, e.g. as input to TR profiling or genotyping from haplotype-resolved BAMs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-consensus-generation--4e80cf801be725ca))
- **long-read-consensus-generation**: Use when raw nanopore amplicon reads (2D or 1D2) from mock or mixed microbial communities are too error-prone (~15% raw error) and a de novo, reference-free full-length 16S consensus with ~99.5% accuracy is the analysis goal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-consensus-generation--edc6c46eff2714f2))
- **long-read-consensus-generation-via-partial-order-alignment**: Use when error-prone haplotype-resolved long-read alignments (BAM) over candidate repetitive regions must be condensed into accurate per-haplotype consensus sequences before motif/multiplicity profiling or downstream comparison to GRCh38. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-consensus-generation-via-partial-order-alignment))
- **long-read-consensus-polishing**: Use when you have basecalled nanopore FASTQ/FASTA reads for a small genome (e.g. Phage Lambda, E. coli, M. tuberculosis) and need a de novo assembly whose error rate quantifies basecaller quality against a known reference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-consensus-polishing--05f321b59e98b56e))
- **long-read-consensus-polishing**: Use when a DBG2OLC (or similar) hybrid assembly backbone has been produced and base-level errors from high-error-rate third-generation reads (up to ~40% ONT MinION error) must be corrected before final assembly evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-consensus-polishing--5226bc3c514fff27))
- **long-read-consensus-polishing**: Use when a long-read (nanopore) genome assembly shows consensus-level base errors — e.g. low BUSCO completeness (3.0% complete / 92.9% missing in the uncorrected eel scaffolds) — and Illumina paired-end data from the same species is available for polishing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-consensus-polishing--395df6aee1a9f160))
- **long-read-contig-alignment**: Use when evaluating how much of a HiFi read set a metagenome assembly (e.g. metaMDBG, metaflye, hifiasm-meta, or a user-submitted assembly.fasta) actually captures, via read-to-contig alignment ratios. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/long-read-contig-alignment))
- **long-read-contig-anchoring-and-layout**: Use when you need to scaffold/assemble a genome from high-error long reads (e.g. ONT MinION up to ~40% error, PacBio) by combining them with accurate Illumina DBG contigs, producing a backbone for later consensus polishing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-contig-anchoring-and-layout))
- **long-read-contig-to-assembly-mapping**: Use when you need to independently verify that assembled SV haplotype consensus contigs are real by aligning them to a finished diploid assembly and checking locus/orientation concordance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-contig-to-assembly-mapping))
- **long-read-corrector-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating how a long-read corrector's performance changes with input error rate or ploidy, e.g., simulated PacBio CLR triploid E. coli reads at 15% vs. 5% error. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-corrector-benchmarking))
- **long-read-coverage-downsampling**: Use when you must quantify how much long-read coverage an SV caller needs to match recall/precision, by comparing caller F1 against a GIAB truth set across a downsampled HiFi coverage series. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-coverage-downsampling))
- **long-read-coverage-subsampling**: Use when you have a deep long-read dataset (e.g., PacBio) plus matching short reads and need to determine at what coverage an assembler's contiguity (NGA50) plateaus or improves most. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-coverage-subsampling--1b3fb778446b3870))
- **long-read-coverage-subsampling**: Use when assessing whether an SV caller retains recall/precision/F1 and genotype accuracy as long-read coverage drops, e.g. subsampling HG002 Hifi/CLR/ONT BAMs to 10x to compare against assembly-based baselines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-coverage-subsampling--617fcbc2da2631ea))
- **long-read-dataset-handling**: Use when the input is a position-sorted, BX-tagged SLR bam (e.g. 10X HG002 GRCh37) optionally paired with PacBio HiFi bam/FASTQ, and the goal is indel/SV (&gt;50 bp) calling or runtime/memory benchmarking against long-read SV callers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-dataset-handling))
- **long-read-demultiplexing**: Use when noisy ONT long-read FASTQ/FASTA single-cell data needs cell-barcode demultiplexing or barcode discovery, e.g. 10x Chromium libraries where barcodes contain sequencing errors and a whitelist may or may not be known. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/long-read-demultiplexing))
- **long-read-dovetail-alignment-filtering**: Use when long reads must join strain-separated contigs into scaffolds: you have a strain-oblivious/strain-separated contig FASTA, a long-read FASTQ, and need dovetail read-to-contig links to scaffold within a strain-resolved pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/long-read-dovetail-alignment-filtering))
- **long-read-error-aware-variant-calling**: Use when calling within-genome 16S gene copy variants from full-length PacBio amplicon reads (e.g., B. vulgatus OTUs at 99% identity) and substitution frequencies must be separated from platform-specific indel errors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/long-read-error-aware-variant-calling))
- **long-read-error-correction**: Use when correcting raw Nanopore R9/R10 or PacBio CLR diploid long reads prior to assembly and heterozygous alleles (rather than only consensus accuracy) must be preserved. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-error-correction--742d35d1228620e3))
- **long-read-error-correction**: Use when raw PacBio CLR or ONT long reads (FASTA/FASTQ, gzip allowed) from diploid, polyploid, or metagenomic mixed samples need error correction that preserves low-frequency haplotype variants prior to assembly or QV benchmarking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-error-correction--001dc1fc450cb2a6))
- **long-read-error-tolerance-interpretation**: Use when evaluating k-mer-based metagenome comparison tools on simulated long-read (PacBio) datasets — e.g. the CAMI 'toy' HMP samples — where you must judge whether clustering-by-origin errors stem from the error model rather than the algorithm. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/long-read-error-tolerance-interpretation))
- **long-read-fragment-extraction-pacbio**: Use when preparing PacBio SMRT (or other long-read) BAM alignments plus a position-sorted, bgzipped VCF as input for diploid haplotype phasing with SpecHap; the phaser consumes fragments, not alignments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/long-read-fragment-extraction-pacbio))
- **long-read-fragment-preprocessing**: Use when preparing Hi-C proximity-ligation read data for diploid haplotype assembly with SpecHap, and trans-homolog interactions (inferred insertion &gt; 40 Mbp) must be removed from fragment input. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/long-read-fragment-preprocessing))
- **long-read-genome-alignment**: Use when long nanopore reads or RCA-derived consensus sequences must be mapped to a reference genome (e.g. hg19) to identify back-splice junctions and full-length circRNA isoforms from isoCirc data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-genome-alignment--2b281e9d7803575d))
- **long-read-genome-alignment**: Use when raw nanopore long-read FASTQ/BAM data must be aligned to a reference genome before variant calling, and the analysis goal is SV/CNV detection where aligner choice affects call sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-genome-alignment--8cec567f57b0cb03))
- **long-read-genome-alignment-minimap2**: Use when PacBio long-read (Iso-Seq style) RNA-seq reads must be mapped to a reference genome as the long-read half of a hybrid assembly, prior to merging with short-read alignments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-genome-alignment-minimap2))
- **long-read-genome-assembly**: Use when the goal is to regenerate or produce a polished genome assembly from nanopore long reads (optionally with Illumina short reads for correction/polishing) using a reproducible, end-to-end pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/long-read-genome-assembly))
- **long-read-genome-assembly**: Use when raw or corrected PacBio/ONT long reads and a reference genome are available and genome assemblies must be generated and compared across correction methods, or when polishing raw long-read assemblies is required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-genome-assembly--0cfbf5821062c8ae))
- **long-read-genome-assembly**: Use when assembling low-coverage (~5x) PacBio HiFi reads de novo and a high-quality reference genome (e.g., CHM13 v2.0) is available to partition reads chromosome-by-chromosome before assembly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-genome-assembly--98436c557fb0bae1))
- **long-read-genome-assembly**: Use when you have long-read FASTQ/FASTA datasets (e.g. HG002 PacBio HiFi or CycloneSEQ G400-ER) and need a de novo whole-genome assembly benchmarked on contiguity and accuracy against a reference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-genome-assembly--98b6153d7395a38a))
- **long-read-genome-assembly**: Use when assembling a diploid genome from long noisy reads (PacBio CLR or Nanopore R9/R10 FASTQ/FASTA) and haplotype-resolved, haplotype-specific contigs are needed without parental/trio data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-genome-assembly--539d4236438ec4e0))
- **long-read-genome-assembly**: Use when you have long reads (e.g. Nanopore FASTQ) plus optionally paired short reads from a bacterial isolate and need a preliminary long-read-only assembly to classify contigs and strip chromosomal reads before plasmid-focused hybrid assembly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/long-read-genome-assembly))
- **long-read-genome-assembly-pipeline-execution**: Use when you have HG002-style long-read BAM inputs (PacBio HiFi/CLR or ONT) and need to run a phased diploid assembly pipeline and quantify its computational resource usage (memory, walltime, CPU hours) versus hifiasm or Flye+HapDup. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-genome-assembly-pipeline-execution))
- **long-read-genome-assembly-quality-assessment**: Use when a set of corrected long reads (e.g., HECIL output LongRead_Corrected.fasta) must be benchmarked against baselines using assembly-based metrics (N50 via QUAST, dnadiff accuracy) plus k-mer and alignment metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-genome-assembly-quality-assessment))
- **long-read-genome-scaffolding**: Use when a draft assembly (FASTA contigs) needs improved contiguity and matching long reads (e.g. ONT FASTQ such as SRR10028109) are available to provide mapping-supported contig joins. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-genome-scaffolding))
- **long-read-genomic-rearrangement-analysis**: Use when an unresolved rare-disease case is suspected to harbor a large-scale structural rearrangement and you have long-read sequencing data (ONT or PacBio) already aligned to a reference genome in BAM format. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-genomic-rearrangement-analysis))
- **long-read-genomics**: Workflow for nanopore or PacBio long-read QC, alignment, polishing, methylation-aware analysis, and structural variant discovery. ([BioTender-max/awesome-bio-agent-skills](https://github.com/BioTender-max/awesome-bio-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/bioclaw_hub/long-read-genomics) · ★224 · 3 copies)
- **long-read-genomics-benchmarking**: Use when two long-read methylation/modification callers must be compared on the same aligned HiFi BAMs, with per-CpG read-level predictions scored against BS-seq labels to produce accuracy/sensitivity/specificity/AUC tables. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-genomics-benchmarking))
- **long-read-genomics-starter**: Use this starter when a task lands in the `Long-read genomics` frontier leaf and the repository has curated resources but no dedicated runtime implementation yet. ([ma-compbio-lab/SkillFoundry](https://github.com/ma-compbio-lab/SkillFoundry/tree/HEAD/skills/genomics/long-read-genomics-starter) · ★46)
- **long-read-genotyping**: Use when you have a long-read bam/cram plus its reference genome and a fasta of insertion sequences (e.g. Alu, L1, SVA, HERV) and need to call non-reference TE insertions with haplotype/genotype support in VCF. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-genotyping))
- **long-read-ground-truth-validation**: Use when you need to validate or benchmark a short-read single-cell TE quantification method and matched long-read data (nanopore scNanoRNAseq or PacBio) from the same sample exists to serve as ground truth at the TE subfamily level. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/long-read-ground-truth-validation))
- **long-read-haplotype-assembly**: Use when you have a bgzipped, tabix-indexed VCF plus a sorted fragment file (10X/Hi-C, PacBio SMRT, ONT, or paired-end NGS) and need fast, memory-efficient diploid phasing, especially for error-prone third-generation long reads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/long-read-haplotype-assembly))
- **long-read-haplotype-phasing**: Use when ONT long reads carry methylation tags (MM/ML) and existing SNV-only phase blocks from HapCUT2 or WhatsHap need to be joined into longer autosomal phase blocks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-haplotype-phasing))
- **long-read-haplotype-phasing**: Use when you have a strain-oblivious long-read metagenome assembly (FASTA) plus a coordinate-sorted indexed BAM of long reads and need to separate reads/scaffolds by strain using haplotype phasing rather than short reads or references. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/long-read-haplotype-phasing))
- **long-read-heterozygous-snp-calling**: Use when you must identify heterozygous SNP sites from Nanopore/PacBio long reads aligned to haplotype-collapsed contigs (e.g., to phase reads or flag inconsistent overlaps in a diploid string-graph assembly). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-heterozygous-snp-calling))
- **long-read-hla-typing**: Use when you have long-read WGS FASTQ data (ONT PromethION or PacBio HiFi) from a sample with unknown HLA type and need group-level HLA-I/II predictions using a single Unix pipeline command. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-hla-typing))
- **long-read-host-depletion**: Use when the input is a single-end ONT long-read FASTQ(.gz) from a microbial sample contaminated with human host sequence, and reads aligning to the host genome must be removed or the resource cost benchmarked. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/long-read-host-depletion))
- **long-read-host-identification-evaluation**: Use when choosing a host-assignment strategy (e.g., Kraken2, Centrifuger, MetaMaps, MEGAN-LR, minimap2 variants, read-overlapping) for ARG-carrying long reads and you need quantitative misclassification benchmarks before deployment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-host-identification-evaluation))
- **long-read-hybrid-error-correction**: Use when noisy long reads (e.g. PacBio E. coli, S. cerevisiae, or heterozygous A. funestus flowcell data) must be corrected with matched Illumina short reads, and alignment/assembly-based correction quality metrics are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-hybrid-error-correction))
- **long-read-indel-genotyping**: Use when long-read alignments (BAM) must be genotyped for a predefined panel of InDel sites supplied as a BED configure file, applying homopolymer (default length 4) and Q15 base-quality filters, or when benchmarking InDel calls against DeepVariant at targeted loci. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-indel-genotyping))
- **long-read-insertion-sequence-characterization**: Use when tumor/control paired long-read (ONT or PacBio) data contain somatic insertion calls whose full-length inserted sequences must be classified by mobile element type and characterized for truncation, inversion, transduction, and source-element properties. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-insertion-sequence-characterization))
- **long-read-isoform-assignment-quality-verification**: Use when molecule consensus BAMs from Nanopore scRNA-seq must be assigned to annotated Gencode isoforms and the resulting isoform-level UMI count matrices need verification against reported assignment rates and per-cell medians. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-isoform-assignment-quality-verification))
- **long-read-isoform-block-structure-parsing**: Use when you have isocirc.out records from isoCirc long-read circRNA catalogs (12 human tissues + HEK293) and need to recover exact exonic compositions and count internal alternative splicing events within circRNAs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-isoform-block-structure-parsing))
- **long-read-isoform-consensus-repair**: Use when Nanopore single-cell/spatial long reads are grouped into UMI clusters and you must fix misalignment or end truncation before isoform quantification, e.g. false-positive small internal exons or truncated read ends from high sequencing error. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/long-read-isoform-consensus-repair))
- **long-read-isoform-database-querying**: Use when you have a populated isoSeQL SQLite database (isoform, counts, isoform_ends, ends_counts, sampleData, exp tables) and need to compare isoforms, counts, or splice junction ends across multiple Iso-Seq samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-isoform-database-querying))
- **long-read-isoform-detection**: Use when BAM/GFF3/BED6 alignments of long reads (PacBio Iso-Seq or ONT cDNA/direct RNA) from a targeted gene, minigene, or SIRV spike-in must be resolved into isoforms and quantified without prior splice-site annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/long-read-isoform-detection))
- **long-read-isoform-quality-control**: Use when you have a collapsed representative isoform FASTA from long-read single-cell isoform sequencing (e.g., HIT-scISOseq) and need SQANTI3 structural classification plus rules filtering to quantify retained isoforms per sample. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-isoform-quality-control))
- **long-read-isoform-quantification**: Use when Nanopore single-cell/spatial FASTQ reads (e.g., Jurkat GSP-targeted, MOB Visium, CRCLM) must be turned into cell-by-isoform count matrices, or when benchmarking isoform quantification methods against Illumina or PacBio IsoSeq gold standards. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/long-read-isoform-quantification))
- **long-read-isoform-read-count-tracking**: Use when you have SQANTI3 classification.txt output for multiple Iso-Seq samples whose PB.X.Y isoform IDs cannot be matched across samples, and you need a cross-sample isoform/read-count database that can absorb new samples on later runs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-isoform-read-count-tracking))
- **long-read-isoform-variable-end-analysis**: Use when deciding how a collapsing/merging tool (isoseq collapse, gffcompare) treats transcripts with identical intron chains but variable 5'/3' ends, e.g., when variable ends across samples risk being discarded into a single full-length isoform entry. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-isoform-variable-end-analysis))
- **long-read-isoseq-validation-analysis**: Use when benchmarking computationally predicted IPA/polyA sites from conventional RNA-seq (e.g., InPACT, IPAFinder) against long-read Iso-seq isoform annotations to obtain validation rates for a dataset such as GSE167486 small airway epithelial cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/long-read-isoseq-validation-analysis))
- **long-read-macrohaplotype-calling**: Use when input is a long-read (e.g., PacBio HiFi) read-reference BAM and targeted STR/SNP/InDel configure files, and you need read-backed phased multi-allelic macrohaplotypes — including variants (private mutations) not listed in the configure files. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-macrohaplotype-calling))
- **long-read-mapper-benchmarking**: Use when comparing long-read mappers on datasets containing inversion structural variants, needing sensitivity/precision/accuracy tables (e.g., Table 1 style) or wall-clock runtime rankings across coverage levels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-mapper-benchmarking))
- **long-read-mapping-qc-statistics**: Use when a nanoranger (5p10XGEX) run processes multimeric long reads (e.g., PacBio 15-mer MAS-seq arrays or natural ONT multimers) and you must confirm deconcatenation succeeded before downstream genotyping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-mapping-qc-statistics))
- **long-read-mapping-to-reference-genome**: Use when mapping long noisy PacBio or Oxford Nanopore reads against a large reference genome and inversion structural variant calls are desired, e.g. NA12878-style datasets in FASTA/FASTQ with a reference genome.fa. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-mapping-to-reference-genome))
- **long-read-masseq-data-retrieval**: Use when a benchmark or pipeline evaluation requires real PacBio MAS-Seq / Kinnex-Iso-Seq long-read single-cell cDNA reads (e.g. Revio PBMC runs) and no local FASTQ/BAM input exists yet. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-masseq-data-retrieval))
- **long-read-metagenome-assembly**: Use when starting from raw long reads (PacBio .fq.gz or ONT) of a low-complexity metagenome and you need a strain-oblivious assembly plus indexed read alignment as input to strain-resolved assembly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/long-read-metagenome-assembly--32ea4d2191ac1ea4))
- **long-read-metagenome-assembly**: Use when assembling a metagenome from a metagenomic dataset that has at least one short-read paired-end Illumina library plus long reads (ONT or PacBio) or mate-pair links, and you need NGA50/accuracy benchmarking against assemblers like metaFlye or OPERA-MS. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/long-read-metagenome-assembly--aa14c18a9f8cf520))
- **long-read-metagenome-binning**: Use when binning long-read (e.g. PacBio/ONT) assembled contigs into MAGs and you want the ensemble clustering strategy that recovers more high-quality bins than any single-epsilon DBSCAN run or second- binner. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-metagenome-binning))
- **long-read-metagenome-strain-separation**: Use when a low-complexity single-sample long-read metagenome dataset (PacBio BAM or Oxford Nanopore BAM, '--nanopore' flag for ONT) needs strain-resolved assemblies rather than a single consensus, e.g., to quantify coverage of individual reference strains like L. helveticus NWC_2_3/NWC_2_4. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/long-read-metagenome-strain-separation))
- **long-read-metagenomic-quality-control**: Use when raw Nanopore or PacBio long-read metagenomic FASTQs must be cleaned before ARG profiling (e.g., Argo/SARG+), including removal of yeast-mapped and unmapped reads from mock communities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-metagenomic-quality-control))
- **long-read-metagenomic-read-mapping**: Use when you need strain-/species-level assignment and sample composition from long-read (PacBio/ONT) metagenomic FASTQ data, especially with reads ≥1000 bp and a comprehensive reference database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/long-read-metagenomic-read-mapping))
- **long-read-methylation-phasing**: Use when you need haplotype-phased, allele-specific CpG methylation from PacBio HiFi/CCS reads, especially across repetitive regions (RepeatMasker repeats, segmental duplications, cenSats) where short-read BS-seq fails. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-methylation-phasing))
- **long-read-ont-pacbio**: Use when processing ONT or PacBio reads: basecalling, alignment, small variants, SV, phasing, methylation, assembly, or run metrics. Not for run pass or fail (run-qc-and-demultiplexing) or short-read SV (sv-cnv-calling). ([oumarkme/research_agents](https://github.com/oumarkme/research_agents/tree/HEAD/plugins/bioinfo-lab/skills/long-read-ont-pacbio))
- **long-read-overlap-detection**: Use when you have a FASTQ/FASTA file of long reads (PacBio, ONT, BGI CycloneSEQ) and need to build an overlap graph for de novo assembly or benchmark overlap detection against tools like minimap2, MECAT2, MHAP, wtdbg2, xRead, or BLEND. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-overlap-detection))
- **long-read-overlap-graph-construction**: Use when quality-controlled ONT/PacBio reads flagged as ARG-containing need to be grouped into clusters (shared genomic origin) before species-level ARG host assignment, e.g., inside the Argo workflow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-overlap-graph-construction--96e414568d578d7e))
- **long-read-overlap-graph-construction**: Use when pairwise overlaps of long reads must be converted into a labeled, weighted bidirectional graph (nodes=read IDs, edges=overlaps) to support chromosome class correction or read binning, e.g., low-coverage HiFi reference-guided assembly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-overlap-graph-construction--ef548f3c380fe492))
- **long-read-overlap-layout-assembly**: Use when assembling a genome from low-coverage (10x–20x) long erroneous reads (PacBio/3GS) together with preassembled high-coverage (e.g., 50x) Illumina DBG contigs, and N50 far beyond short-read-only assembly is the goal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-overlap-layout-assembly))
- **long-read-overlap-scoring**: Use when assembling large genomes from long erroneous 3GS reads (PacBio/ONT) hybridized with accurate DBG contigs, and you need to find overlapping read pairs without all-vs-all base-level alignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-overlap-scoring))
- **long-read-phasing-output-parsing**: Use when a VolcanoSV-asm phasing output leaves unphased reads in a long-read BAM and you need to bin every read to a haplotype before haplotype-aware local assembly or phased SV calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-phasing-output-parsing))
- **long-read-phasing-workflow-configuration**: Use when phasing diploid individuals from PacBio SMRT, ONT, Hi-C, 10x linked-reads, or paired-end NGS data and you need to build SpecHap, extract fragments with ExtractHair, and run the phaser on position-sorted bgzipped VCF plus fragment files. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/long-read-phasing-workflow-configuration))
- **long-read-pileup-allele-counting**: Use when calling SNVs from sorted, indexed BAM/CRAM files of error-prone single-molecule long reads and you need to vet candidate variants (e.g. from allelotyping or an input VCF) for context-specific Nanopore strand-bias artifacts prior to haplotype assembly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-pileup-allele-counting))
- **long-read-preprocessing-for-plasmid-assembly**: Use when a pooled or subsampled ONT long-read FASTQ must be length- and quality-filtered before Plassembler plasmid assembly, or when reproducing Plassembler's chopper defaults of 500 bp / Q9. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/long-read-preprocessing-for-plasmid-assembly))
- **long-read-qc**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-/tree/HEAD/Skills/Genomics/long-read-sequencing/long-read-qc) · ★36 · 3 copies)
- **long-read-qc**: Plan and verify Oxford Nanopore or PacBio HiFi metagenomic read QC, including raw diagnostics, length/quality filtering, adapter removal, host depletion, and before-versus-after yield gates. Use when selecting NanoPlot, Chopper, Filtlong, HiFiAdapterFilt, or Hostile without applying short-read assumptions to long reads. ([PKU-EMBL/Metagenomics-Skills](https://github.com/PKU-EMBL/Metagenomics-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/long-read-qc) · ★7)
- **long-read-qc-gptomics-bioskills**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/analysis/long-read-qc-gptomics-bioskills) · ★745)
- **long-read-qc-metrics-computation**: Use when you have aligned PacBio LRTS BAM samples (e.g. CTL/VPA hepatocyte groups) already imported into an IsoTools Transcriptome object and need to quantify what fraction of reads are affected by technical artifacts such as truncation, RTTS, or internal priming. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-qc-metrics-computation))
- **long-read-qc-metrics-interpretation**: Use when raw nanopore FASTQ reads need QC before AMR or assembly analysis in NanoGalaxy, or when per-read length, Phred quality, and yield summary statistics must be reported. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-qc-metrics-interpretation))
- **long-read-qc-summarization**: Use when raw Nanopore FASTQ reads enter the nf-core/mag long-read pre-processing subworkflow and per-sample QC reports are needed before and after adapter trimming, control decontamination and Filtlong quality filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/long-read-qc-summarization))
- **long-read-quality-filtering**: Use when raw Oxford Nanopore long-read FASTQ (and matched Illumina FASTQ) needs trimming and quality/length filtering before de novo plasmid or genomic assembly in NanoGalaxy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-quality-filtering))
- **long-read-quality-filtering**: Use when raw or decontaminated Oxford Nanopore FASTQ reads must be quality-filtered (Filtlong) and QC-reported (NanoPlot) before long-read or hybrid metagenome assembly in nf-core/mag. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/long-read-quality-filtering))
- **long-read-read-alignment**: Use when you have C-to-T (*_CT.fq.gz) and G-to-A (*_GA.fq.gz) converted nanopore FASTQ reads plus a converted reference (output.fa from convert_ref.py) and need read-to-genome alignments before -alignment selection and methylation calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-read-alignment))
- **long-read-read-alignment-to-custom-hla-reference**: Use when predicting HLA class I/II alleles from raw long-read WGS FASTQ data and a SAM-with-MD-tag alignment compatible with HLAminer's stream mode is needed as input. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-read-alignment-to-custom-hla-reference))
- **long-read-read-simulation**: Use when a benchmark needs controlled long-read metagenome communities — e.g. varying strain divergence, strain number, or coverage depth — and real reads cannot provide known ground-truth strain genomes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/long-read-read-simulation))
- **long-read-recall-assessment-against-assembly**: Use when evaluating how many k-mers and synthetic mate-pair read pairs from raw or Lordec-corrected long reads are recovered against an Illumina assembly, e.g. benchmarking alignment-free library extraction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-recall-assessment-against-assembly))
- **long-read-reference-alignment**: Use when you have long reads (FASTA/FASTQ, e.g. PacBio CLR or ONT) from raw or error-corrected pipelines and need to map them to reference genomes to measure residual error rates or validate correction performance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-reference-alignment--db6b7bd2bc7cf269))
- **long-read-reference-alignment**: Use when raw per-sample long-read FASTQ files (PacBio HiFi or targeted ONT) must be aligned to a human reference genome FASTA to yield coordinate-sorted, indexed BAM/CRAM files as the basis for phasing, variant calling, or assembly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-reference-alignment--bc3336646eac9b0c))
- **long-read-reference-alignment-parsing**: Use when low-coverage HiFi reads (e.g., ~5x human/plant datasets) must be assigned to reference chromosomes or scanned for split mappings spanning different chromosomes before de novo chromosome-by-chromosome assembly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-reference-alignment-parsing))
- **long-read-rna-seq-alignment**: Use when you have raw long-read RNA FASTQ data (PacBio Iso-Seq, ONT) and unspliced reference sequences (FASTA) and need spliced alignments (BAM+BAI) as input for VIsoQLR isoform analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/long-read-rna-seq-alignment))
- **long-read-rna-seq-feature-analysis**: Use when a trained random forest model (e.g., dsRID trained on /data/Pacbio_AD_data.tsv) needs feature-level interpretation, specifically to rank features like Skip_ratio and Len_skip by permutation contribution score. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/long-read-rna-seq-feature-analysis))
- **long-read-rnaseq-window-scanning**: Use when the input is PacBio long-read RNA-seq BAM alignments and the goal is a genome-wide scan for candidate endogenous dsRNA regions that feed into novel-long-dsRNA and structured-RNA classification (e.g. reproducing dsRID Fig. 3A counts). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/long-read-rnaseq-window-scanning))
- **long-read-sam-bam-alignment-filtering**: Use when aligned long-read SAM/BAM files must be quality-controlled before variant-feature extraction: reads with alignment quality &lt;20 or FLAG 4 (&gt;256) must be removed prior to CIGAR-based deletion analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-sam-bam-alignment-filtering))
- **long-read-scaffold-reference-alignment**: Use when you must confirm whether a single scaffold from a hybrid metagenome assembly corresponds to a known reference genome and judge its structural completeness versus fragmented competitor assemblies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/long-read-scaffold-reference-alignment))
- **long-read-scaffolding-graph-construction**: Use when you have long nanopore reads (mixed ONT chemistries) plus many short unique seed sequences (all exactly the same length, numeric unique IDs excluding 0), and need a de novo genome scaffold graph without expensive whole-read assembly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-scaffolding-graph-construction))
- **long-read-self-correction**: Use when you have FASTA/FASTQ long reads (PacBio or ONT) with raw per-base error rates (e.g. ~12%) and need corrected reads as input for assembly or downstream variant/coverage analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-self-correction))
- **long-read-sequence-identity-clustering**: Use when long reads identified as telomeric (PacBio HiFi WGS or telobait-captured FASTQ/BAM) must be grouped by their subtelomeric region so per-end analyses such as TVS signature plots can be built for specific telomeres. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-sequence-identity-clustering))
- **long-read-sequencing**: Configure and test Oxford Nanopore long-read verification, instrument turnaround (TAT), and escalation from routine modalities. Use when editing long_read_sequencing config, long_read_sequencing_params.json, instrument_turnaround.json, or crusher_labs/modalities/long_read_sequencing.py. ([bckirkup/Crusher_to_the_Bridge](https://github.com/bckirkup/Crusher_to_the_Bridge/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/long-read-sequencing) · ★1)
- **long-read-sequencing**: Apply Long Read Sequencing in computational biology and life sciences. Use when analyzing biological data, developing therapeutics, or building bioinformatics pipelines. This skill covers methodologies, tools, and applications in long read sequencing. ([Sandeeprdy1729/skill_galaxy](https://github.com/Sandeeprdy1729/skill_galaxy/tree/HEAD/skills/bio/long-read-sequencing) · ★1)
- **long-read-sequencing-agent**: --&gt; ([FreedomIntelligence/OpenClaw-Medical-Skills](https://github.com/FreedomIntelligence/OpenClaw-Medical-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/long-read-sequencing-agent) · ★3.1k · 13 copies)
- **long-read-sequencing-agent**: AI-powered analysis of long-read sequencing data (PacBio, ONT) for structural variant detection, isoform discovery, epigenetic modifications, and de novo assembly. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/long-read-sequencing-agent) · ★104 · 4 copies)
- **long-read-sequencing-clair3-variants**: Deep learning-based variant calling from long reads using Clair3 for SNPs and small indels. Use when calling germline variants from ONT or PacBio alignments, particularly when high accuracy is needed for clinical or research applications. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/long-read-sequencing-clair3-variants) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **long-read-sequencing-isoseq-analysis**: Analyze PacBio Iso-Seq data for full-length isoform discovery and quantification. Use when characterizing transcript diversity or identifying novel splice variants. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/long-read-sequencing-isoseq-analysis) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **long-read-sequencing-nanopore-methylation**: Calls DNA methylation from Oxford Nanopore sequencing data using signal-level analysis. Use when detecting 5mC or 6mA modifications directly from nanopore reads without bisulfite conversion. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/long-read-sequencing-nanopore-methylation) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **long-read-sequencing-pipeline-execution**: Use when PacBio Iso-Seq subread BAM inputs must be converted into full-length transcript isoform annotations (e.g., a merged BED12 genome annotation) via the reproducible nf-core/isoseq workflow rather than ad-hoc per-tool scripts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-sequencing-pipeline-execution))
